```js
  let count = 0
```

ASSISTANT: Closures capture lexical scope so inner functions can access variables defined in outer functions even after they return.
--- INSTRUCTION ---
```js
function counter() {
  let count = 0
  return () => ++count
```
```js
}
```

```js
  console.log('shutdown gracefully')
})
```

USER: How does nullish coalescing differ from || in production?

USER: How do microtasks and macrotasks affect execution order in production?
ASSISTANT: Microtasks run before the next repaint, while macrotasks run after the current JavaScript stack clears.
--- INSTRUCTION ---
```js
Promise.resolve().then(() => console.log('microtask'))
setTimeout(() => console.log('macrotask'), 0)
console.log('sync')
if (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
```

```js
setTimeout(() => console.log('macrotask'), 0)
console.log('sync')
```

```js
  console.log('skipping frequent request')
}
```

USER: How does memoization speed up repeated calculations in production?
ASSISTANT: Cache function results so repeated calls with the same arguments reuse the computed value.
--- INSTRUCTION ---
```js
const cache = new Map()
function fib(n) {
  if (cache.has(n)) return cache.get(n)
  const value = n < 2 ? n : fib(n - 1) + fib(n - 2)
  cache.set(n, value)
  return value
}
if (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
  console.log('production ready')
}
```

```js
  if (cache.has(n)) return cache.get(n)
  const value = n < 2 ? n : fib(n - 1) + fib(n - 2)
  cache.set(n, value)
  return value
}
```
```js
process.on('SIGTERM', () => {
  console.log('shutdown gracefully')
})
```
--- RESPONSE ---

USER: How do map, filter, and reduce differ in production?
ASSISTANT: map transforms items, filter selects items, and reduce folds items into a single result.
--- INSTRUCTION ---
```js
const nums = [1, 2, 3]
const doubled = nums.map(n => n * 2)
const evens = nums.filter(n => n % 2 === 0)
const sum = nums.reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0)
```

USER: How do I validate JSON payloads safely with error boundaries?
ASSISTANT: Parse JSON inside try/catch and verify the expected shape before using the data.
--- INSTRUCTION ---
```js
try {
  const body = JSON.parse(req.body)
  if (typeof body.name !== 'string') throw new Error('Invalid body')
} catch (err) {
  res.status(400).send('Invalid JSON')
}
try {
  doWork()
} catch (error) {
  console.error('boundary caught', error)
}
```

USER: How do I store records in IndexedDB in production?
ASSISTANT: Open a database transaction and add records through an object store.
--- INSTRUCTION ---
```js
const request = indexedDB.open('app', 1)
request.onsuccess = () => {
  const db = request.result
  const tx = db.transaction('store', 'readwrite')
}
if (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
  console.log('production ready')
}
```

```js
}
```
